The proposed action constraint functions as an implicit regularization, which counteracts the plasticity loss typically caused by aggressive gradient updates. This preservation of network adaptability enhances sample efficiency, particularly in scenarios with a high update-to-data ratio, and accelerates the early training process. In contrast to prior fast reinforcement learning approaches that rely on discrete value distributions, our method utilizes a continuous Gaussian representation equipped with adaptive variance regulation, which improves value estimation accuracy by sampling confident and informative transitions. Extensive experiments on MuJoCo Playground and HumanoidBench demonstrate that FastDSAC not only stabilizes the overall training process but also achieves superior asymptotic performance and faster convergence compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [187] [RCT: A Robot-Collected Touch-Vision-Language Dataset for Tactile Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31694)
*Jingbo He,Michael Färber,Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了RCT数据集，用于评估触觉感知对未见材料的泛化能力，揭示了接触序列和材料留出评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要处理开放世界中的物体，其触觉表示必须能够泛化到训练时未见过的材料。然而，当前的评估方法可能由于数据划分不当而过于乐观，未能真实反映模型在新材料上的泛化性能。

Method: 研究者构建了RCT（Robotic Contact Tactile）数据集，这是一个由机器人收集的、包含触觉、视觉和语言信息的大规模数据集。数据集记录了机器人按压122种工业参考材料（7个类别）时的接触序列，并保留了序列信息。基于此，研究者设计了严格的数据划分策略，包括移除接触序列重叠和留出未见材料，以进行更真实的评估。

Result: 实验发现，如果评估时接触序列存在重叠（即同一物理交互的帧同时出现在训练集和测试集），会严重高估模型性能（Recall@1下降17.7个百分点）。当进一步在训练时完全留出某些材料类别时，模型在这些未见材料上的性能急剧下降（Recall@1仅约25.1%）。这表明模型对新材料的泛化能力有限。

Conclusion: RCT数据集及其评估协议为机器人触觉感知提供了一个可重复、更真实的基准，特别是能够评估对未见材料的泛化能力。研究结果明确指出，新材料泛化是机器人触觉感知领域亟需解决的核心挑战。

Abstract: For robots manipulating open-world objects, tactile representations must generalize to unseen materials. We introduce RCT (Robotic Contact Tactile), a robot-collected touch-vision-language dataset with 29,279 tactile frames from full robot presses on 122 industrial reference materials in 7 categories, recorded with three DIGIT sensors at multiple contact positions. RCT preserves each press as a contact sequence, enabling held-out evaluation across materials, categories, sensors, contact positions, and contact sequences. Frames from one press are strongly correlated: frame-random splits can place near-duplicate observations of the same physical interaction in both training and test. With the encoder held fixed, removing contact-sequence overlap reduces tactile-to-text Recall@1 by 17.7 percentage points. When materials are additionally held out at training time, performance drops sharply, leaving held-out-material Recall@1 at 25.1 +/- 6.1% averaged over three held-out draws. The public TVL/HCT split shows the same structure: every test contact sequence appears in training, and raw-pixel nearest neighbors recover the correct sequence in 98.3% of cases. Uniformly sampling a press improves contrastive training, and RCT-trained embeddings improve category probes on unseen materials. RCT makes contact-sequence-aware, held-out-material evaluation reproducible and exposes novel-material generalization as a central challenge for robotic tactile perception. The RCT dataset is open-sourced at https://faerber-lab.github.io/RCT/

</details>


### [188] [UniTacVLA: Unified Tactile Understanding and Prediction in Vision Language Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31723)
*Xidong Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Jiaxin Shi,Fucai Zhu,Siyu Zhu,Michael Yu Wang,Xiaojun Wu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的触觉学习框架，通过将触觉信号建模为动态交互线索，显著提高了接触丰富操作任务的成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在接触丰富的灵巧操作任务中性能有限，而近期的视觉-触觉-语言-动作（VTLA）方法虽然引入了触觉信号，但将其视为被动辅助输入，难以建模触觉语义和未来物理交互。

Method: 提出统一的触觉学习框架，构建统一的触觉潜在空间，通过触觉思维链推理和粗到细的未来触觉预测，联合建模当前触觉状态和未来接触变化，形成状态感知和动力学感知的触觉先验。基于此先验，引入触觉-动作混合控制器，结合实时和预测的触觉反馈，用高频校正优化低频动作块。

Result: 在四类接触丰富的任务（调整、插入、擦拭、装配）的真实世界实验中，包括干净和外部扰动设置，该方法在成功率、操作精度和接触鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提高了接触丰富操作任务的性能，通过将触觉信号建模为动态交互线索，实现了更灵巧的物理交互，证明了其在机器人操作领域的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in many robotic manipulation tasks, yet remain limited in contact-rich dexterous manipulation. To overcome this limitation, recent vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) methods incorporate tactile sensing into VLA models to provide direct contact information. However, they typically treat tactile signals as passive auxiliary inputs, making it difficult to model tactile semantics and future physical interactions. To this end, we propose a unified tactile learning framework for contact-rich manipulation that models tactile signals as dynamic interaction cues for both contact understanding and prediction. Specifically, we construct a unified tactile latent space and jointly model current tactile states and future contact changes through tactile chain-of-thought reasoning and coarse-to-fine future tactile prediction, thereby forming a state-aware and dynamics-aware tactile prior. Based on this prior, we introduce a tactile-action mixed controller that combines real-time and predicted tactile feedback to refine low-frequency action chunks with high-frequency corrections. Real-world experiments on four categories of contact-rich tasks, including adjustment, insertion, wiping, and assembly, under both clean and externally perturbed settings, show that our method improves success rate, manipulation accuracy, and contact robustness over existing methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in dexterous physical interaction.

</details>


### [189] [Autonomous UAV Navigation for Individual Wildlife Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31772)
*Claire Sun,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Jenna Kline*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向个体重识别的自主无人机导航系统，通过集成YOLOv11和DINOv2姿态分类器实时优化飞行策略，以采集适用于个体识别的高质量图像，并在斑马等物种上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野生动物个体重识别对种群监测、行为追踪和保护政策评估至关重要，但大规模数据收集仍依赖人工，劳动密集。现有无人机系统多优化群体行为视频，无法满足个体识别模型对特定图像质量（如清晰的侧面图案）的要求。

Method: 设计自主无人机导航系统，集成YOLOv11进行动物检测，DINOv2-based姿态分类器引导实时飞行决策（检测动物、调整方向以暴露侧面、根据最小边界框阈值接近目标），使图像采集直接服务于下游重识别任务。

Result: 通过在肯尼亚采集的斑马影像进行案例研究，验证了系统的可行性；该方法适用于其他具有诊断性表面图案的物种（如长颈鹿、老虎、大象），展示了其泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个任务感知的具身AI框架，用于生态数据收集，其中下游重识别需求驱动实时感知与控制，为自动化、高质量野生动物个体识别数据采集提供了新途径。

Abstract: Reliable individual re-identification (re-ID) of wildlife is essential for population monitoring, behavioral tracking, and conservation policy evaluation, yet large-scale data collection remains labor-intensive, relying on manual efforts by ecologists or citizen scientists. We propose an autonomous drone navigation system that actively optimizes image capture for downstream re-ID, moving beyond passive aerial sensing. The system combines YOLOv11 object detection with a DINOv2-based pose classifier to guide real-time flight decisions: detecting animals, orienting to expose the lateral flank (the surface of interest for pattern-based re-ID), and approaching until the subject meets a minimum bounding-box threshold. Unlike prior drone systems that optimize for group-level behavioral video, ours targets the specific image-quality requirements of individual-identification models. We demonstrate feasibility through a case study on zebra using footage collected in Kenya, and show the approach generalizes to other species with diagnostic surface patterns, including giraffes, tigers, and elephants. Our work establishes a framework for task-aware embodied AI for ecological data collection, in which downstream re-ID requirements drive real-time perception and control.

</details>


### [190] [Reinforcement Learning-Based Control for an Inline Skating Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31807)
*Ethan Marot,Thomas Bi,Clemens Schwarke,Victor Klemm,Marco Hutter,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过强化学习训练人形机器人进行被动直排滑冰，实现了比步行能耗降低50%的高效移动策略，并成功转移到真实机器人硬件上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将日益动态的人形机器人与强化学习相结合，以解决被动直排滑冰中复杂、欠驱动的力学问题。将人形机器人的多功能敏捷性与人类滑冰者使用的高速、高能效移动策略相结合。

Method: 训练并部署一个强化学习控制策略，使经过改装、装备消费级直排滑冰轮（而非传统足部）的人形机器人能够执行新颖的移动策略。通过在训练和验证期间使用不同的几何轮模型（球形和椭球形）来克服被动轮的固有不稳定性和仿真接触伪影，并使用定制的成功导向命令课程和专门的滚动奖励，而无需依赖人类运动数据、模仿学习或运动学先验。

Result: 该策略实现了对滑板的精确6自由度控制，能够执行动态的、由边缘驱动的推进策略。与标准步行步态相比，运输成本（CoT）降低了高达50%。该策略成功实现了到物理Booster T1硬件的零样本迁移。实际部署展示了动态平衡、抵抗主动物理扰动的能力，以及能够高速转弯的敏捷移动策略。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，通过强化学习，可以使人形机器人掌握被动直排滑冰技能，从而实现高效、敏捷的移动方式。所学习的策略不仅在仿真中有效，还能成功应用于真实机器人硬件，展示了该方法在实现复杂动态运动方面的潜力。

Abstract: As humanoid robots become increasingly dynamic, coupling them with reinforcement learning offers a promising approach to solving the complex, underactuated mechanics of passive inline skating. Equipping a humanoid robot with passive inline skating wheels presents an opportunity to combine the versatile agility of humanoids with the high-speed, energy-efficient locomotion strategies utilized by human skaters. In this paper, we train and deploy a reinforcement learning control policy that enables novel locomotion strategies for a humanoid robot modified to equip consumer inline skates instead of conventional feet. Unlike previous work limited to quadrupedal robots or actively driven wheels, our system allows for precise 6-DoF control of the skates to execute dynamic, edge-driven propulsion strategies. Our skating strategies emerge entirely from our reward structure, without reliance on human motion data, imitation learning, or kinematic priors. We overcome the inherent instability of passive wheels and simulation contact artifacts by utilizing different geometric wheel models (spherical and ellipsoidal) during training and validation, along with a custom success-based command curriculum and a specialized rolling reward. Consequently, our policy demonstrates up to a 50% reduction in Cost of Transport (CoT) compared to standard walking gaits. The resulting policy successfully transfers zero-shot to the physical Booster T1 hardware. Real-world deployments demonstrate dynamic balance, the ability to reject active physical perturbations, and agile locomotion strategies capable of turning at speed. A video of our results can be found at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-_APcOS7uFo.

</details>


### [191] [RoboTacDex: A Dexterous Visual-Tactile-Action Dataset for Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31836)
*Xinyi Wang,Donghan Li,Zi'Ang Chen,Chong Yu,Chen Xin,Peng Ye,Yingkai Sun,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RoboTacDex，一个大规模、多模态、多样化的灵巧操作数据集，旨在通过高质量演示轨迹提升机器人的操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人学习领域，大规模、多样化的演示轨迹是提升机器人操作能力的基础。现有的数据集可能缺乏足够的规模、模态多样性或任务挑战性，不足以支持复杂、类人操作任务的学习。因此，构建一个涵盖多任务、多技能、多物体交互，且包含丰富传感信息（视觉、触觉）的高质量数据集至关重要。

Method: 研究基于公开的人形机器人Unitree G1构建了RoboTacDex数据集。该数据集包含6000条轨迹，覆盖19个任务、23项技能，并与22个物体进行交互。其核心特点包括：1）提供多视角RGB和深度图像、触觉反馈及详细语义标注等多模态数据；2）包含需要双臂和灵巧手协同完成的复杂任务，以模拟人类操作逻辑和真实世界的复杂性；3）开发了改进的多摄像头同步系统，以实现毫秒级的多模态数据同步记录。

Result: 研究者在所构建的数据集上评估了三种具有代表性的模仿学习模型，分析了它们在不同任务类别上的性能以及各自的优势和局限性。成功的试验结果和在一系列任务中展现出的适度泛化能力，证明了所收集数据集的有效性和多样性。

Conclusion: RoboTacDex是一个为机器人灵巧操作学习设计的大规模、高质量、多模态数据集。它通过涵盖多样化的挑战性任务和提供全面的传感记录，为训练和评估更强大的机器人操作模型提供了重要基础。该数据集将很快开源。

Abstract: In the field of robot learning, large-scale and diverse demonstration trajectories provide the fundamental basis for enhancing robotic manipulation ability. We introduce RoboTacDex, a large, multi-modal, and diverse dataset of dexterous manipulation behaviors performed with a humanoid robot. Built on the publicly accessible humanoid robot Unitree G1, RoboTacDex consists of 6k trajectories covering 19 tasks, 23 skills, and interactions with 22 objects. RoboTacDex provides comprehensive records including multi-view RGB and depth information, tactile feedback, and detailed semantic annotations. Furthermore, the dataset features a variety of relatively challenging tasks that can only be completed by dual arms and dexterous hands, aiming to mimic human-like operational logic and simulate real-world manipulation complexity. To ensure data collection quality, we develop an improved multi-camera synchronization system to enable millisecond data synchronization and recording of modalities. In our experiments, we evaluate three representative imitation learning models on our dataset,
  analyzing their performance as well as their respective strengths and limitations across different task categories. Successful trial results and a moderate level of generalization capabilities across a suite of tasks indicate the effectiveness and diversity of the collected dataset. Our dataset will be open-sourced soon.

</details>


### [192] [Bridging Local Observation and Global Simulation in Closed-Loop Traffic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31844)
*Ziyan Wang,Tan Xiang,Peng Chen,Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT框架通过自监督失败发现和偏好引导的测试时对齐，解决自动驾驶模拟中基于局部日志训练的模拟器在全局闭环环境中因上下文不匹配而产生的不现实行为问题，显著减少碰撞和交通违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当基于以自我为中心的驾驶日志训练的自回归流量模拟器部署在全局可观测的闭环环境中时，会因日志中周围智能体仅部分被观测到而产生本地到全局的上下文不匹配。这导致模拟器学习不完整的上下文-动作映射，在闭环推演中暴露，引发异常停止、不安全交互和规则违反等不现实行为。

Method: 提出CRAFT（Contextual pReference Alignment Framework for Traffic Simulation）框架，其核心是：1）将基础模拟器作为全局可观测沙盒，从日志初始状态生成多样化的“what-if”推演以自监督发现上下文导致的失败；2）用人类对齐的驾驶先验对失败进行基础化，转化为偏好监督数据；3）训练一个上下文偏好评估器（CPE）；4）在推理时，CPE作为插件模块，在完整场景上下文下对候选动作评分并重新加权自回归解码，以生成全局一致的行为。

Result: CRAFT在不重新训练基础模拟器的情况下，将碰撞减少了31.2%，交通违规减少了33.2%。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过自监督失败发现和偏好引导的测试时对齐，有效缓解了自动驾驶模拟中局部日志训练到全局闭环部署的上下文偏差，显著提升了模拟行为的真实性和安全性。

Abstract: A local-to-global context mismatch arises when autoregressive traffic simulators trained on ego-centric driving logs are deployed in globally observable closed-loop environments. In such logs, the ego vehicle has rich local observations, while surrounding agents are only partially observed due to perception limits and occlusions. As a result, simulators may learn incomplete context--action mappings that remain hidden in log-based training but emerge during closed-loop rollouts, leading to unrealistic behaviors such as abnormal stops, unsafe interactions, and rule violations. We propose CRAFT, a Contextual pReference Alignment Framework for Traffic Simulation, to mitigate this mismatch via self-supervised failure discovery and preference-guided test-time alignment. CRAFT treats the base simulator as a globally observable sandbox, generating diverse what-if rollouts from logged initial states to expose context-induced failures. These failures are grounded with human-aligned driving priors and converted into preference supervision for training a Contextual Preference Evaluator (CPE). At inference time, CPE acts as a plug-in alignment module that scores candidate actions under complete scene context and reweights autoregressive decoding toward globally coherent behaviors. CRAFT mitigates this local-to-global contextual bias, reducing collisions by 31.2\% and traffic violations by 33.2\% without retraining the base simulator.

</details>


### [193] [Z-1: Efficient Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31846)
*Lang Cao,Renhong Chen,Luyi Li,Peng Wang,Mofan Peng,Yitong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Z-1，一种针对基于流的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的强化学习后训练框架。该框架仅使用公开的RoboCasa演示进行监督微调，然后采用任务级的组相对策略优化（GRPO）方法，在24个标准RoboCasa任务上实现了平均80.6%的成功率，比监督微调初始化提高了13.2个百分点，超越了现有的最先进模型。结果表明，系统化的GRPO后训练可以在无需额外私有演示的情况下显著提升基于流的VLA策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操作策略大多局限于行为克隆或从固定演示中进行监督微调（SFT），这限制了策略从自身失败中学习和改进的机会。本文旨在解决这一问题，提出一种强化学习后训练框架，使策略能够通过在线交互和自我优化来提升性能。

Method: Z-1框架基于π0.5构建，首先使用公开的RoboCasa演示进行监督微调（SFT），随后在24个标准RoboCasa任务上应用任务级的组相对策略优化（GRPO）策略。为提高在线优化的效率和稳定性，该框架结合了共享前缀轨迹构建、树状轨迹分支、完成感知的奖励校准以及VLM和动作专家的选择性联合训练等技术。

Result: Z-1在全部24个RoboCasa任务上实现了平均80.6%的成功率，相比其监督微调初始化基线提高了13.2个百分点，并超越了已发表的最先进模型。这表明，系统化的GRPO后训练能够显著提升基于流的VLA策略的性能，且无需依赖额外的私有演示数据。

Conclusion: 系统化的GRPO后训练是一种有效的方法，能够显著提升基于流的VLA策略在机器人操作任务中的性能。该方法仅依赖公开的演示数据进行初始监督微调，通过强化学习后训练让策略从自身交互经验中学习和改进，从而超越了依赖大量私有演示数据的现有最先进模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising framework for robotic manipulation by connecting language instructions, visual observations, and continuous control. However, most existing policies remain limited by behavior cloning or supervised fine-tuning (SFT) from fixed demonstrations, which provides limited opportunity to improve from the policy's own failures. In this paper, we present Z-1, a reinforcement learning (RL) post-training framework for flow-based VLA models. Built on top of $π_{0.5}$, Z-1 uses only publicly released RoboCasa demonstrations for SFT and then applies a task-wise Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) strategy across $24$ standard RoboCasa tasks. To improve the efficiency and stability of online optimization, Z-1 combines shared-prefix rollout construction, tree-structured trajectory branching, completion-aware reward calibration, and selective joint training of VLM and Action Expert. Across all $24$ RoboCasa tasks, Z-1 achieves an average success rate of $80.6\%$, improving over its SFT initialization by $13.2\%$ points and outperforms the published sota models. These results show that systematic GRPO post-training can substantially improve flow-based VLA policies without additional private demonstrations.

</details>


### [194] [Improving path-tracking performance of an articulated tractor-trailer system using a non-linear kinematic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31889)
*Marina Murillo,Guido Sanchez,Nestor Deniz,Lucas Genzelis,Leonardo Giovanini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于铰接式拖拉机-拖车系统的非线性数学模型，结合滚动时域技术，可显著改善路径跟踪性能，尤其适用于精准农业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接式拖拉机-拖车系统因具备双重转向机构，在精准农业的小田地播种、喷洒和收割作业中具有重要应用价值，但需要更有效的控制方法以提升拖车路径跟踪精度。

Method: 建立铰接式拖拉机-拖车系统的非线性数学模型，并将其嵌入非线性模型预测控制器中，同时考虑拖车运动学特性，利用ROS和Gazebo仿真平台进行验证。

Result: 所提出的模型和控制方法能显著减少拖车在直线路径和地头转弯时的位置偏差，实现对拖车本身位置的精确控制。

Conclusion: 该非线性数学模型与模型预测控制相结合的方法能有效提升铰接式系统的路径跟踪性能，为精准农业中的自主作业提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel non-linear mathematical model of an articulated tractor-trailer system that can be used, in combination with receding horizon techniques, to improve the performance of path tracking tasks of articulated systems. Due to its dual steering mechanisms, this type of vehicle can be very useful in precision agriculture, particularly for seeding, spraying and harvesting in small fields. The articulated tractor-trailer system model was embedded within a non-linear model predictive controller and the trailer position was monitored. When the kinematic of the trailer was considered, the deviation of trailer's position was reduced substantially alongside not only straight paths but also in headland turns. Using the proposed mathematical model, we were able to control the trailer's position itself rather than the tractor's position. The Robot Operating System (ROS) framework and Gazebo simulator were used to perform realistic simulations examples.

</details>


### [195] [CoDex: Learning Compositional Dexterous Functional Manipulation without Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31909)
*Bowen Jiang,William Painter Reger,Roberto Martin-Martin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In this work, we study Compositional Dexterous Functional Object Manipulation (CD-FOM): tasks such as aiming and actuating a spray bottle on a plant or a glue gun on wood, which require both actuating an object's internal mechanism and controlling its pose to apply the object's function to the environment. These tasks pose significant challenges for robots due to the demanding integration of semantic understanding of the object's function, actuation mode, and application area with intricate physical dexterity to manage grasp stability, movement trajectory, and actuation. We introduce CoDex, a zero-demonstration framework that autonomously discovers CD-FOM manipulation strategies. CoDex uses vision-language models (VLMs) to infer semantic constraints from the task and scene. These constraints guide analytic constrained optimization to generate a short list of functional grasp candidates that can be efficiently refined with reinforcement learning to generate full grasp-move-actuate policies transferable from simulation to the real world. We evaluate CoDex on a 7-DoF robot arm with a 16-DoF multi-fingered hand across six CD-FOM tasks involving previously unseen objects with internal mechanisms, including spray bottles, hot glue guns, air dusters, flashlights, and pepper grinders, and their application to unseen target objects, showcasing its ability to autonomously discover and execute complex, physically viable dexterous behaviors without human demonstrations. More information at https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/CoDex/.

</details>


### [196] [Learning Locomotion on Discrete Terrain via Minimal Proximity Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31912)
*Jiale Fan,Connor Flynn,Tianao Xu,Junzhe He,Andrei Cramariuc,Marco Hutter,Robert Baines*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在四足机器人足部集成低成本、高频率红外接近传感器的方法，提供“预接触”反馈，使机器人能够预判地形不连续性，从而增强在非结构化地形上的导航能力。该方法计算成本低、抗遮挡，并通过强化学习实现仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人动态运动控制依赖的全球感知系统（如LiDAR、深度相机）存在延迟、遮挡和高计算成本问题，而本体感觉反馈是被动的，无法提前应对地形变化。因此，需要一种低延迟、低计算成本且能提前感知地面接触的局部感知方法，以提升机器人在复杂、非结构化地形中的鲁棒性。

Method: 研究通过在四足机器人足部嵌入红外接近传感器，获取局部、高频的“预接触”信号。这些信号被集成到强化学习框架中，用于训练机器人预判地形不连续性（如间隙、踏脚石）。整个系统设计注重低成本、低功耗和实时性，并通过仿真到现实的迁移验证其有效性。

Result: 实验表明，足部红外传感器提供的稀疏、近场信号可以在仿真中可靠建模，并高保真度地迁移到现实世界。在离散地形（如间隙和踏脚石）上的测试证明，该局部接近传感系统显著提升了机器人通过地形的鲁棒性，为不可预测环境提供了一种低功耗、低延迟的感知方案。

Conclusion: 基于足部的接近传感为解决非结构化地形导航中的感知难题提供了一种轻量级、高效且鲁棒的方案。它不仅可以作为复杂全局感知系统的低成本补充或替代，而且通过强化学习框架实现了从仿真到现实的可靠迁移，展示了局部、高频传感在机器人运动控制中的潜力。

Abstract: Learning-based control has revolutionized dynamic locomotion, yet navigating unstructured terrain remains limited by a robot's incomplete awareness of imminent ground contact. While global perception systems such as LiDARs and depth cameras provide environmental context, they are frequently plagued by latencies, occlusions, and the high computational cost of dense geometric reconstruction. On the other hand, proprioceptive feedback is purely reactive, initiating corrections only after impact has occurred. This work explores embedding a minimal suite of low-cost, high-frequency infrared proximity sensors directly into the feet of a quadrupedal robot. These sensors provide "pre-contact" feedback that is robust to self-occlusions and significantly less computationally demanding than conventional vision-based pipelines. By integrating these localized signals into a reinforcement learning framework, we enable the robot to anticipate terrain discontinuities such as gaps and stepping stones that are problematic for traditional perception stacks due to occlusions or state estimation drift. We demonstrate that such sparse, near-field sensing can be reliably modeled in simulation and transferred to the real world with high fidelity. Experimental results show that local proximity sensing substantially improves traversal robustness over discrete terrain and offers a low-power, low-latency alternative or complement to complex global perception suites in unpredictable environments. For more information about results and methods, please see the project website: https://sites.google.com/view/foot-tof/home.

</details>


### [197] [MVP-Nav: Multi-layer Value Map Planner Navigator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31919)
*Wenyuan Xie,Shaokai Wu,Yijin Zhou,Yanbiao Ji,Guodong Zhang,Bayram Bayramli,Qiuchang Li,Xunchu Zhou,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVP-Nav是一个针对零样本物体目标导航（ZSON）的物理感知RGB-only导航框架。它通过重建显式物理占用和引入多层价值地图（MVM），解决了仅有RGB输入时的物理不确定性和语义-物理不匹配问题，在基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本物体导航（ZSON）方法存在根本缺陷：它们要么依赖缺乏几何基础的高层语义推理，要么学习缺乏明确物理约束的端到端策略，导致语义上合理但物理上不安全的行为。核心动机在于，如何在仅使用RGB图像（无显式深度信息）的条件下，通过引入结构化的物理先验知识，来弥补深度传感器的缺失，并实现安全、准确的导航。

Method: 本文提出了MVP-Nav框架，其核心方法包括：1) 利用3D基础模型，将单目RGB观测中的2D语义实例投影并重建为3D定向边界框，从而构建显式的全局空间语义占用表示。2) 引入多层价值地图（MVM），该地图将高层语义优先级（例如目标物体的相关性）与重建的底层几何信息融合到一个共享的成本空间中，从而支持在物理约束下进行几何规划。

Result: 在多个零样本物体导航基准测试上的大量实验表明，MVP-Nav的性能显著优于现有的无深度（depth-free）方法，并达到了 state-of-the-art 的水平。这验证了其核心假设：结构化的物理先验能够有效补偿主动深度传感器的缺失。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，通过显式重建物理占用并将语义推理与物理约束对齐，仅使用RGB感知的智能体也能实现强大且物理安全的导航性能。MVP-Nav框架为解决ZSON中的物理不确定性问题提供了一个有效的解决方案，推动了低感知成本（无深度传感器）自主导航技术的发展。

Abstract: Zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ZSON) with RGB-only perception poses a fundamental challenge for embodied agents, as the absence of explicit depth information introduces severe physical uncertainty and semantic-physical misalignment. Existing approaches either rely on high-level semantic reasoning without geometric grounding or learn end-to-end policies that lack explicit physical constraints, often resulting in semantically plausible but physically unsafe behaviors. In this paper, we propose MVP-Nav, a physical-aware RGB-only navigation framework that aligns perception, planning, and control with the real 3D world. MVP-Nav reconstructs explicit physical occupancy from monocular observations by leveraging 3D foundation models to project 2D semantic instances into 3D oriented bounding boxes, forming a global spatial semantic representation. To unify high-level semantic reasoning and low-level physical constraints, we introduce a Multi-layer Value Map (MVM) that integrates semantic priorities and reconstructed geometry into a shared cost space, enabling physically grounded geometric planning. Extensive experiments on zero-shot object navigation benchmarks demonstrate that MVP-Nav significantly outperforms existing depth-free methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and validating that structured physical priors can effectively compensate for the absence of active depth sensors.

</details>


### [198] [LeCropFollow: Latent Space Planning for Navigation in Unstructured Crop Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31941)
*Felipe Tommaselli,Francisco Affonso,Arthur Pompeu,Gianluca Capezzuto,Arun Narenthiran Sivakumar,Girish Chowdhary,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对农业机器人在非结构化导航特征（如不规则种植、种植间隙）下现有几何方法易失败的问题，本文提出了LeCropFollow视觉导航框架。该框架绕过显式几何建模，通过整合自监督语义热图提取器与基于模型的强化学习规划器（TD-MPC2），直接在学习到的潜在流形上优化轨迹，保留了关键的语义上下文信息。实验表明，该系统能实现从仿真到物理世界的零样本迁移，并在玉米田复杂场景中显著降低导航失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何导航方法在农业机器人中处理非结构化特征（如不规则行距、种植间隙）时存在根本缺陷：它们将高维视觉数据压缩为确定性的空间参考，从而丢弃了应对模糊地形所必需的不确定性和语义上下文信息，导致在复杂环境下导航失败率高。

Method: 提出LeCropFollow视觉导航框架，核心是绕过显式几何建模，采用学习到的潜在表示。具体方法包括：1. 集成一个自监督的语义热图提取器，用于从视觉数据中提取富含语义的特征。2. 将提取的语义热图输入TD-MPC2（一种先进的基于模型的强化学习规划器），直接在压缩的潜在流形上进行轨迹优化。整个系统处理未经压缩的热图信号，完整保留了语义上下文。

Result: 1. 该框架的表示学习方法成功实现了从简化模拟环境到真实物理世界的零样本迁移，无需进行任何微调。2. 在玉米生长后期田间的大量实地实验中，LeCropFollow在非结构化行间导航性能与现有最佳基线相当，但在存在种植间隙的场景中表现显著更优。与基于关键点的方法相比，其将语义导航失败率降低了2.4倍。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，基于潜在空间的规划方法为在异质化、非结构化的农业环境中进行机器人导航提供了一种比传统几何估计更稳健、更有效的替代方案。该方法通过保留和利用语义上下文信息，显著提高了机器人在复杂真实场景中的导航鲁棒性。

Abstract: Unstructured navigational features, such as irregular planting or discontinuities, remain the primary failure mode for under-canopy agricultural robots. Existing geometric approaches often fail in these scenarios because they compress high-dimensional visual data into deterministic spatial references, effectively discarding the uncertainty and semantic context required to navigate ambiguous terrain. To address this, we present LeCropFollow, a visual navigation framework that bypasses explicit geometric modeling in favor of a learned latent representation. By integrating a self-supervised semantic heatmap extractor with TD-MPC2, a Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) planner, our system optimizes trajectories directly within a latent manifold. The framework operates over the uncompressed heatmap signal, preserving the semantic context that geometric reductions discard. We demonstrate that this representational shift enables zero-shot transfer from simplified simulation to the physical world without fine-tuning. Extensive field experiments in late-stage corn fields show that LeCropFollow matches state-of-the-art baselines in unstructured rows but significantly outperforms them in plantation gaps, achieving a 2.4x reduction in semantic failures compared to keypoint-based methods. These results suggest that latent planning offers a robust alternative to geometric estimation for operations in heterogeneous agricultural environments. Code, models, and data available: https://felipe-tommaselli.github.io/lecropfollow .

</details>


### [199] [RRT-Rope: A deterministic shortening approach for fast near-optimal path planning in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31948)
*Louis Petit,Alexis Lussier Desbiens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RRT-Rope的新方法，旨在大规模三维环境中（如地下矿井）为无人机快速找到近最优路径。该方法通过改进的RRT-connect算法快速生成可行路径，并利用一种确定性的“捷径”技术进行后处理，从而在路径成本和计算时间上显著优于其他流行的RRT变体算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的许多路径规划算法（如RRT及其变体）在处理大规模、开阔的三维环境时，存在路径成本高和计算时间长的问题。虽然改进的RRT算法（如Informed RRT*、RRT#）能够找到更优的路径，但它们收敛到最优解需要较长时间。因此，需要一种既能快速找到可行路径，又能高效优化路径质量的新方法。

Method: RRT-Rope算法分为两步：1）使用一个改进的RRT-connect算法快速计算出一条可行路径；2）利用一个确定性的“捷径”技术对这条路径进行快速后处理。该捷径技术的独特之处在于，它利用了树的每个分支中添加的中间节点，从而更有效地缩短路径。

Result: 通过仿真和统计分析表明，RRT-Rope在所有测试的仿真环境中，在路径成本和计算时间方面均优于其他流行的RRT变体和路径缩短技术。在具有代表性的矿井场景中，其计算速度比次优算法快达70%。

Conclusion: RRT-Rope是一种高效的新算法，它结合了快速路径生成与高效的路径优化，特别适用于大规模、开阔的三维环境。该算法能在显著缩短计算时间的同时，找到路径成本更低的近最优解，为无人机在此类环境中的路径规划提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Many path planning algorithms have been introduced so far, but most are costly, in path cost and in processing time, in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments such as underground mining stopes explored by an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) algorithms are popular because of their probabilistic completeness and rapidity in finding a feasible path in single-query problems. Many of the algorithms (e.g. Informed RRT*, RRT#) developed to improve RRT need considerable time to converge in large environments. Shortcutting an RRT is an old idea that has been proven to outperform RRT variants. This paper introduces a new method, RRT-Rope, that aims at finding a near-optimal solution in a drastically shorter amount of time. The proposed approach benefits from fast computation of a feasible path with an altered version of RRT-connect, and post-processes it quickly with a deterministic shortcutting technique, taking advantage of intermediate nodes added to each branch of the tree. This paper presents simulations and statistics carried out to show the efficiency of RRT-Rope, which gives better results in terms of path cost and computation time than other popular RRT variations and shortening techniques in all our simulation environments, and is up to 70% faster than the next best algorithm in a representative stope.

</details>


### [200] [Adapting Generalist Robot Policies with Semantic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31958)
*Jagdeep Singh Bhatia,Andrew Wagenmaker,William Chen,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SARL通过优化语言提示空间来利用通用机器人策略的预训练技能，从而实现对复杂长时任务的高效在线适应，显著优于传统强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习方法直接优化动作分布，要求基础策略的初始动作分布接近最优，这对于复杂或长时任务（超出预训练分布）效果不佳。需要一种能更有效利用通用策略预训练技能的适应方法。

Method: 提出语义操作强化学习（SARL），将通用策略视为可控技能先验，通过在线交互学习优化语言提示空间，从而调用和组合策略已有的技能来解决新任务。

Result: 在真实世界环境和模拟基准中，SARL解锁了视觉语言动作模型的新能力，使其能够适应解决复杂长时任务，并且在改善机器人部署行为方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SARL提供了一种有效途径，通过优化语言提示空间来充分利用预训练通用策略的技能先验，实现对复杂机器人任务的高效、语义有意义的在线适应，推动了机器人行为适应技术的发展。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies learn a diverse repertoire of behaviors from large-scale pretraining. In principle, this makes them excellent priors for downstream adaptation via reinforcement learning (RL). In practice, however, standard RL methods leveraging this prior optimize directly over robot actions, requiring the base policy's action distribution to be close to that of a performant policy from the start. This assumption breaks down for complex or long-horizon tasks that fall outside the pretraining distribution. Our key insight is that, for sufficiently expressive generalist policies, language prompts are an effective alternative space for learning to solve such tasks: modulating language inputs elicits skills already within the policy's repertoire, which can be composed to solve tasks beyond its zero-shot capabilities. We propose Semantic Action Reinforcement Learning (SARL), which learns to optimize this prompt space through online interaction, treating the generalist policy as a controllable skill prior. Importantly, leveraging pretrained skills rather than learning new ones from scratch yields structured, semantically meaningful exploration and highly efficient online improvement, and learning to modulate prompts through experience grounds them in induced real-world behaviors for robust task-solving. Across real-world settings and simulated benchmarks, we show SARL unlocks fundamentally new capabilities -- adapting VLA behavior to solve complex, long-horizon tasks -- and significantly outperforms existing approaches for improving robot behavior in deployment.

</details>


### [201] [OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31993)
*Arnav Balaji,Arpit Bahety,Sriniket Ambatipudi,Daniel Lam,Junhong Xu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了OOPSIEVERSE，一个用于损伤感知家庭机器人操作的统一仿真框架和基准测试，旨在通过提供明确、物理基础且任务无关的损伤信号，解决机器人部署中的物理安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器人操作能力快速发展，但物理安全仍是部署家庭机器人的主要障碍：任务成功不足以避免机器人自身或周围环境的损坏。仿真提供了无害的替代方案，但现有仿真器缺乏检测、量化和表示损伤的通用机制。

Method: OOPSIEVERSE包含两个核心部分：(1) DAMAGESIM，一个仿真器无关的框架，用于在导航和操作过程中检测和量化损伤，它将接触力、温度变化、液体相互作用等来源转化为相应的机械、热或流体损伤；(2) 一套家庭任务，旨在评估常见损伤模式，并区分任务完成与安全执行。作者通过在OmniGibson和RoboCasa两个不同物理后端的仿真器中实例化DAMAGESIM来展示其通用性。

Result: 研究表明，OOPSIEVERSE在多个用例中具有实用性：(1) 通过实时损伤反馈指导更安全的演示收集；(2) 通过基于损伤的模仿学习和强化学习学习更安全的操作策略；(3) 对最先进的视觉语言动作策略的安全性进行基准测试；(4) 改善模拟到真实世界策略的安全性。结果凸显了该框架的通用性和实用性。

Conclusion: OOPSIEVERSE作为一个开源基础，为系统化、可扩展的安全机器人操作研究提供了潜力，它通过提供统一的损伤感知仿真框架和基准，促进了更安全机器人行为的研究和开发。

Abstract: While robotic manipulation capabilities have advanced rapidly, physical safety remains a major barrier to deploying household robots: task success is insufficient if the robot damages itself or its surroundings. Simulation offers a harm-free alternative to costly and dangerous real-world training and evaluation, yet existing simulators lack general mechanisms to detect, quantify, and represent damage. To address this gap, we introduce OOPSIEVERSE, a unified simulation framework and benchmark for damage-aware household manipulation. OOPSIEVERSE provides damage as an explicit, physically-grounded, and taskagnostic signal by converting sources such as contact forces, temperature changes, and liquid interactions into corresponding mechanical, thermal or fluid damage. OOPSIEVERSE comprises two core elements: (1) DAMAGESIM, a simulator-agnostic framework for detecting and quantifying damage during navigation and manipulation, and (2) a suite of household tasks designed to evaluate common damage modes and distinguish between task completion and safe execution. We demonstrate the generality of our framework by instantiating DAMAGESIM in two simulators with different physics backends, OmniGibson (Nvidia Omniverse) and RoboCasa (MuJoCo). We further showcase the utility of OOPSIEVERSE across multiple use cases, including (1) guiding safer demonstration collection via real-time damage feedback, (2) learning safer manipulation policies through damage-conditioned imitation learning and reinforcement learning, (3) benchmarking the safety of state-of-the-art Vision Language Action policies, and (4) improving real-world safety of sim-to-real transferred policies. Together, our results highlight the potential of OOPSIEVERSE as an open-source foundation for systematic, scalable research on safe robot manipulation. For code and more information, please refer to https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/oopsieverse/

</details>


### [202] [Human-as-Humanoid: Enabling Zero-Shot Humanoid Learning from Ego-Exo Human Videos with Human-Aligned Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32009)
*Xiaopeng Lin,Ruoqi Yang,Shijie Lian,Zhaolong Shen,Bin Yu,Changti Wu,Haibao Liu,Yuxiang Zhang,Hong Li,Qiyuan Su,Haochen Liu,Xuguo He,Yukun Shi,Cong Huang,Zhirui Zhang,Bojun Cheng,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“Human-as-Humanoid”框架，旨在将人类的示范动作（通过自我中心和外部视角视频捕获）高效地转换为可执行的人形机器人动作监督，以解决高自由度人形机器人视觉-语言-动作模型训练所需数据稀缺和扩展困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练跨机器人实体的通用视觉-语言-动作模型需要高质量且可扩展的观测-动作数据，但对于高自由度人形机器人而言，此类数据获取困难。遥操作数据与控制接口对齐但效率低；人类自我中心视频包含丰富操作但无法直接作为机器人动作执行。因此，需要一种能将人类演示直接转化为机器人可用监督的方法。

Method: 提出“Human-as-Humanoid”框架，其核心包括：1) 使用PrimeU机器人（一个60自由度、与人对齐的上半身人形）；2) 通过同步的第一人称与第三人称视频，将部署对齐的自我中心观测与第三人称动作捕捉相结合；3) 通过分阶段逆运动学将恢复的人体重建动作重定向为与控制器对齐的60自由度动作块；4) 采用前向运动学感知的监督来训练VLA模型，以保留手腕和指尖的任务空间几何结构。

Result: 实验验证了从动作恢复、机器人动作空间到真实机器人部署的完整转换链。数据分析表明，该框架相比人形遥操作实现了4.8-7.2倍的原始演示吞吐量提升。在多个下游任务上，仅使用转换后人类标签进行后训练的策略，无需目标任务的机器人演示即可泛化到真实机器人部署。

Conclusion: “Human-as-Humanoid”框架成功地将大规模、多样化的人类视觉演示转换为针对高自由度人形机器人的、可执行的观测-动作监督数据。这不仅显著提升了数据收集效率，而且生成的监督数据能有效训练出可直接部署在真实机器人上的策略，为解决人形机器人VLA模型的数据瓶颈提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models across robot embodiments require high-quality observation--action supervision to learn deployable action distributions, yet scaling such robot data remains difficult, especially for high-DoF humanoids. Teleoperation provides controller-aligned supervision, while human egocentric videos capture diverse bimanual manipulation but do not directly provide executable robot actions. We introduce Human-as-Humanoid, a human-to-humanoid supervision framework that enables near-real-time human-centric action generation, making human demonstrations usable for high-DoF humanoid VLA training by jointly aligning the robot embodiment, the sensing setup, and the action-label interface. Built on PrimeU, a human-aligned 60-DoF upper-body humanoid, Human-as-Humanoid uses synchronized ego-exo videos to pair deployment-aligned egocentric observations with exocentric motion recovery, retargets the recovered human motion through staged Inverse Kinematics (IK) into controller-aligned 60-DoF action chunks, and trains the VLA model with Forward Kinematics (FK)-aware supervision to preserve wrist and fingertip task-space geometry. This converts large-scale human demonstrations from visual observations into executable observation--action supervision for the target humanoid. Experiments validate the conversion chain at the motion-recovery, robot-action-space, and real-robot deployment levels. Human-as-Humanoid yields a 4.8--7.2x raw demonstration-throughput gain over humanoid teleoperation in our data-collection analysis, and on several downstream tasks, policies post-trained only with the converted human labels generalize to real-robot deployment without target-task robot demonstrations. The official project website is available at https://zgc-embodyai.github.io/Human-as-Humanoid.

</details>


### [203] [Freeform Preference Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32027)
*Marcel Torne,Anubha Mahajan,Abhijnya Bhat,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自由形式偏好学习(FPL)方法，通过让人类标注者定义自然语言偏好轴（如速度、安全性等）并提供成对偏好，学习语言条件奖励模型和奖励条件策略，在长时域机器人操作任务中显著优于传统方法，并具备行为可组合性与测试时行为引导能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长时域机器人操作任务中，稀疏成功标签提供的信号不足，而二元偏好将多种质量维度压缩为单一模糊信号，这成为奖励设计的主要瓶颈，阻碍了自主机器人策略的改进。

Method: FPL方法允许标注者定义多个自然语言偏好轴（如速度、安全性等），并在每个轴上提供轨迹的成对偏好。这些标注被用来训练一个语言条件奖励模型，该模型将轨迹和偏好标签映射到轴特定奖励。随后，该模型被用于训练一个奖励条件策略，以优化人类指定的多个维度。

Result: 在四个真实世界和两个模拟的长时域操作任务中，FPL相比稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法，性能平均提升了38个百分点。此外，FPL无需显式子任务分割即可学习到稠密的进度信号，展现出数据中未出现的行为组合性，并允许用户在测试时引导策略趋向不同行为而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: FPL通过引入自由形式的多维人类偏好，有效解决了长时域机器人任务中奖励设计的瓶颈问题，不仅显著提升了任务性能，还增强了策略的可解释性、可控性与行为多样性，为基于人类反馈的机器人学习提供了更灵活和强大的框架。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central bottleneck for autonomous robot policy improvement, especially in long-horizon manipulation tasks where sparse success labels provide too little signal and binary preferences collapse many competing notions of quality into one ambiguous signal. We introduce Freeform Preference Learning (FPL), a method for learning robot policies from freeform human preferences. Rather than asking annotators which of two trajectories is better overall, FPL lets them define natural-language preference axes, such as speed, safety, quality of placement, or carefulness, and provide pairwise preferences along each axis. These annotations are used to learn a language-conditioned reward model that maps a trajectory and preference label to an axis-specific reward. We use this model to train a reward-conditioned policy that optimizes across the multiple human-specified dimensions. Across four real-world and two simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, FPL improves over sparse-reward and binary-preference methods by 38 percentage points. Beyond improved performance, FPL learns dense progress signals without explicit subtask segmentation, shows compositionality of behavior not present in the data, and allows users to steer the policy towards different behaviors at test time without retraining. Blog post with videos available at https://freeform-pl.github.io/fpl.website/

</details>


### [204] [DVG-WM: Disentangled Video Generation Enables Efficient Embodied World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32028)
*Ziyu Shan,Zhenyu Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦的视频生成世界模型DVG-WM，用于机器人操控任务，通过将动态学习和视觉合成解耦，显著提升了视频生成质量和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视频的具身世界模型存在一个根本性纠缠：准确的动态建模需要低层次的时间推理，而生成高分辨率视频帧则需要根据高层次语义进行广泛的视觉合成。这种纠缠导致在迭代规划时推理速度过慢，或者预测过于粗糙而无法保留接触丰富的细节，限制了其在机器人操控中的应用。

Method: 提出DVG-WM框架，显式地将世界建模分解为动态学习和视觉合成。给定初始观察和语言指令，模型首先生成一组合理的中间视觉状态序列以预览物理交互，然后细化这些状态以获得高保真视频。采用高效的级联机制，利用流匹配将动态直接映射到视频潜变量，并引入潜变量退化机制以重新生成接触丰富的细节。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和真实世界平台上的实验表明，DVG-WM提高了视频质量，同时实现了高达3.97倍的加速，验证了其作为机器人操控高效具身世界模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将动态建模与视觉合成解耦，DVG-WM有效解决了现有世界模型中的纠缠问题，实现了高质量和高速度的视频生成，为机器人操控提供了高效的具身世界模型基础。

Abstract: Video-based embodied world models provide an appealing substrate for robotic manipulation by predicting future states, yet current approaches remain limited by a fundamental entanglement: accurately modeling dynamics typically requires low-level temporal reasoning, while producing high-resolution frames demands expansive visual synthesis according to high-level semantics. This entanglement results in slow inference speed for iterative planning or too coarse predictions to retain contact-rich details. To solve this dilemma, we present Disentangled Video Generation World Model (DVG-WM), an efficient framework that explicitly decomposes world modeling into dynamics learning and visual synthesis. Conditioned on an initial observation and a language instruction, our model first generates a plausible sequence of intermediate visual states to preview the physical interaction and refines them to obtain high-fidelity videos. Furthermore, an efficient cascading mechanism is proposed, where DVG-WM uses flow matching to directly map the dynamics to video latents, and introduces a latent degradation mechanism to regenerate contact-rich details. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world platforms demonstrate improved video quality with up to 3.97 times acceleration, validating that disentangled video generation can be an efficient embodied world model for robotic manipulation.

</details>
